CREATE TABLE atlanta_crime_data (
	`Crime_ID` INTEGER, 
	`Report_Number` VARCHAR(50) COLLATE utf8mb4_unicode_ci, 
	`Day_Occurred` VARCHAR(20) COLLATE utf8mb4_unicode_ci, 
	`Crime_Date` DATE, 
	`Crime_Time` TIME, 
	`Crime_Type` VARCHAR(100) COLLATE utf8mb4_unicode_ci, 
	`Area_Name` VARCHAR(100) COLLATE utf8mb4_unicode_ci, 
	`Address` LONGTEXT COLLATE utf8mb4_unicode_ci, 
	`Longitude` FLOAT, 
	`Latitude` FLOAT
)ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4 COLLATE utf8mb4_unicode_ci

/*
3 rows from atlanta_crime_data table:

*/

	`Black` INTEGER,

CREATE TABLE food_access (
	`CensusTract` BIGINT, 
	`State` VARCHAR(100) COLLATE utf8mb4_unicode_ci, 
	`County` VARCHAR(100) COLLATE utf8mb4_unicode_ci, 
	`Urban` TINYINT(1), 
	`Pop2010` INTEGER, 
	`Ohu2010` INTEGER, 
	`LILATracts_1And10` TINYINT(1), 
	`LILATracts_halfAnd10` TINYINT(1), 
	`LILATracks_1And20` TINYINT(1), 
	`LILATractsVehicle` TINYINT(1), 
	`HUNVFlag` TINYINT(1), 
	`LowIncomeTracts` TINYINT(1), 
	`PovertyRate` FLOAT, 
	`MedianFamilyIncome` FLOAT, 
	`LA1and10` TINYINT(1), 
	`LAhalfand10` TINYINT(1),

CREATE TABLE us_shootings (
	`IncidentID` INTEGER NOT NULL, 
	`Address` LONGTEXT COLLATE utf8mb4_unicode_ci, 
	`IncidentDate` DATE, 
	`State` VARCHAR(50) COLLATE utf8mb4_unicode_ci, 
	`CityOrCountry` VARCHAR(100) COLLATE utf8mb4_unicode_ci, 
	`VictimsKilled` INTEGER, 
	`VictimsInjured` INTEGER, 
	`SuspectsInjured` INTEGER, 
	`SuspectsKilled` INTEGER, 
	`SuspectsArrested` INTEGER, 
	PRIMARY KEY (`IncidentID`)
)DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4 ENGINE=InnoDB COLLATE utf8mb4_unicode_ci

/*
3 rows from us_shootings table:
IncidentID	Address	IncidentDate	State	CityOrCountry	VictimsKilled	VictimsInjured	SuspectsInjured	SuspectsKilled	SuspectsArrested

*/
